\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{checkmat()}{checkmat()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{convertfort10_8f90_a29aba3f8122a99086f74d817e27d4134} 
subroutine checkmat (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{nelorbj\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{jasmat\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{integer}]{lead}{, }\item[{integer, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{nozeroj\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{integer}]{nnozeroj\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{logical}]{checkall}{, }\item[{integer}]{rank}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{epsr}{, }\item[{logical}]{symmagp}{, }\item[{integer}]{ipf}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Check and validate real Jastrow matrix reconstruction. 

This subroutine validates the reconstruction of a real Jastrow matrix from sparse format. It checks if all non-\/zero elements from the original matrix are properly reconstructed and handles symmetry constraints for AGP and Pfaffian matrices. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelorbj\+\_\+c} & Integer number of Jastrow orbitals \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em jasmat\+\_\+c} & Real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8 Jastrow matrix to be checked and reconstructed \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em lead} & Integer leading dimension of the matrix \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nozeroj\+\_\+c} & Integer array of non-\/zero element indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nnozeroj\+\_\+c} & Integer number of non-\/zero elements \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em checkall} & Logical flag indicating if all elements were properly reconstructed \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em rank} & Integer rank of current process for parallel execution \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em epsr} & Real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8 tolerance for element comparison \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em symmagp} & Logical flag for AGP symmetry \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ipf} & Integer Pfaffian flag (1 for AGP, 2 for Pfaffian) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{checkmat\_complex()}{checkmat\_complex()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{convertfort10_8f90_a1f45c23cfcc3688b7cb77ade13f762a7} 
subroutine checkmat\+\_\+complex (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{nelorbj\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{structcomplex}{complex}}\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}16, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{jasmat\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{integer}]{lead}{, }\item[{integer, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{nozeroj\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{integer}]{nnozeroj\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{logical}]{checkall}{, }\item[{integer}]{rank}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{epsr}{, }\item[{logical}]{symmagp}{, }\item[{integer}]{ipf}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Check and validate complex Jastrow matrix reconstruction. 

This subroutine validates the reconstruction of a complex Jastrow matrix from sparse format. It checks if all non-\/zero elements from the original matrix are properly reconstructed and handles symmetry constraints for AGP and Pfaffian matrices. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelorbj\+\_\+c} & Integer number of Jastrow orbitals \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em jasmat\+\_\+c} & Complex\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}16 Jastrow matrix to be checked and reconstructed \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em lead} & Integer leading dimension of the matrix \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nozeroj\+\_\+c} & Integer array of non-\/zero element indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nnozeroj\+\_\+c} & Integer number of non-\/zero elements \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em checkall} & Logical flag indicating if all elements were properly reconstructed \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em rank} & Integer rank of current process for parallel execution \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em epsr} & Real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8 tolerance for element comparison \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em symmagp} & Logical flag for AGP symmetry \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ipf} & Integer Pfaffian flag (1 for AGP, 2 for Pfaffian) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{dsygv\_my()}{dsygv\_my()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{convertfort10_8f90_a846dfad30f0eabe0c2ccace85f3db0c9} 
subroutine dsygv\+\_\+my (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{ipc}{, }\item[{integer}]{n}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(ipc\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}lda, \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{a}{, }\item[{integer}]{lda}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(ipc\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}ldo, \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{over}{, }\item[{integer}]{ldo}{, }\item[{logical}]{doover}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(ipc\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}n, n)}]{umat}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(n)}]{eigmat}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(n)}]{eig}{, }\item[{integer}]{mine}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{work}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{epsdgel}{, }\item[{logical}]{effham}{, }\item[{integer}]{info}{, }\item[{integer}]{nprocr}{, }\item[{integer}]{rank}{, }\item[{integer}]{comm\+\_\+mpi}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Solve generalized eigenvalue problem with custom handling of small eigenvalues. 

This subroutine solves the generalized eigenvalue problem either\+: a v = e s v (effham=.true.) or a s v = e v (effham=.false.) where \textquotesingle{}s\textquotesingle{} is given in diagonal form s = umat eig umat\texorpdfstring{$^\wedge$}{\string^}dag. Small eigenvalues below epsdgel are zeroed out and excluded from calculation. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ipc} & Integer complex flag (1 for real, 2 for complex) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em n} & Integer dimension of matrices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em a} & Real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8 matrix of dimension (ipc\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}lda,\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em lda} & Integer leading dimension of a \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em over} & Real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8 overlap matrix of dimension (ipc\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}ldo,\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ldo} & Integer leading dimension of over \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em doover} & Logical flag to compute overlap matrix inverse \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em umat} & Real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8 eigenvector matrix \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em eigmat} & Real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8 eigenvalue array \\
\hline

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{findminmax()}{findminmax()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{convertfort10_8f90_a684ca78ae359fe48974f6a5f70b07cb7} 
subroutine findminmax (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{nnozero}{, }\item[{integer, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{nozero}{, }\item[{integer}]{nelorb}{, }\item[{integer}]{imin}{, }\item[{integer}]{imax}{, }\item[{integer}]{len}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Find minimum and maximum indices from a list of non-\/zero elements. 

This subroutine analyzes a list of non-\/zero element indices and finds the minimum and maximum row/column indices. It converts linear indices to 2D matrix indices and determines the range of non-\/zero elements. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nnozero} & Integer number of non-\/zero elements \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nozero} & Integer array containing linear indices of non-\/zero elements \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelorb} & Integer dimension of the matrix (number of orbitals) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em imin} & Integer minimum row/column index found \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em imax} & Integer maximum row/column index found \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em len} & Integer length of the range (imax -\/ imin + 1) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{load\_fort10in()}{load\_fort10in()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{convertfort10_8f90_a037ea349f7ea42389da153204cd17794} 
subroutine convertfort10\+::load\+\_\+fort10in}



Load and initialize data from fort.\+10 input file. 

This subroutine reads the fort.\+10 input file and initializes all necessary data structures for the convertfort10 program. It handles file I/O, memory allocation, parameter initialization, and mesh setup. The subroutine also processes namelist inputs and sets up parallel communication if MPI is enabled.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{purify()}{purify()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{convertfort10_8f90_abbf5f2b8792ce8184d6d4197d0030f85} 
subroutine purify (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nelorbjh, \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{jasmat}{, }\item[{integer}]{nelorbjh}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nelorbj, \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{overj}{, }\item[{integer}]{nelorbj}{, }\item[{logical, dimension(nelorbjh)}]{orbcostn}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Orthogonalize Jastrow matrix with respect to constant orbital. 

This subroutine performs orthogonalization of the Jastrow matrix with respect to the constant orbital. It identifies the constant orbital and adjusts the matrix elements to ensure orthogonality. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em jasmat} & Real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8 Jastrow matrix to be orthogonalized \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelorbjh} & Integer number of Jastrow orbitals (high) \\
\hline

subroutine setorbcost (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{nelorbj\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{jasmat\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{logical, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{orbcostn}{, }\item[{integer, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{nozeroj\+\_\+c}{, }\item[{integer}]{nnozeroj\+\_\+c}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Consolidate multiple constant orbitals into a single one. 

This subroutine handles the case where multiple constant orbitals exist in fort.\+10. It consolidates all constant orbitals into the last one (icost) and rewrites the Jastrow matrix accordingly. This ensures only one constant orbital is used.

Calculate the trace of a matrix product with orbital cost constraints. 

This function computes the trace of the product of overlap matrix and Jastrow matrix, considering only orbitals that are not constant (orbcost = .false.). The trace is calculated as\+: sum\+\_\+\{j,k\} (sum\+\_\+\{i\} \doxylink{namespaceallio_ab6b92097207bb2a7bdb8804e7047833a}{jasmat(i,j)} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} \doxylink{namespaceallio_ab6b92097207bb2a7bdb8804e7047833a}{jasmat(i,k)}) \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} over(j,k) where the sums are restricted to non-\/constant orbitals.
